buf16_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[8, 8, 1, 2], stride=[16, 2, 2, 1])
class op16_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 8, p1: 8, p2: 2}
    index0 = p0
    index1 = p1
    index2 = p2
    index3 = 16*p0 + 2*p1 + p2
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('primals_2', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('primals_2', get_index_1)
        ne = ops.ne(load, load_1)
        to_dtype = ops.to_dtype(ne, torch.int64, src_dtype = torch.bool)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')

    [   MemoryDep('buf105', 64*d0 + 8*d1 + d2, {d0: 12, d1: 8, d2: 8}),
        MemoryDep('buf107', 128*d0 + 15*d1 + d2 + 128*(((15*d1 + d2)//120)) + 8, {d0: 12, d1: 8, d2: 8}),
        MemoryDep('buf110', 8*d0 + 96*d1 + d2, {d0: 12, d1: 8, d2: 8})]

    buf111.layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[1, 12, 8, 1], stride=[96, 8, 1, 96])
    buf111.users = [NodeUser(node=SchedulerNode(name='op113'), can_inplace=False, is_weak=False)]
]
op111.group.device = cuda:0
op111.group.iteration = (96, 8)
op111.sizes = ([12, 8], [8])
buf105_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[12, 8, 8], stride=[64, 8, 1])
buf107_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[12, 8, 16], stride=[128, 16, 1])
buf110_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[8, 12, 8], stride=[96, 8, 1])
primals_3_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.int64, size=[1, 8], stride=[8, 1])
buf111_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[1, 12, 8, 1], stride=[96, 8, 1, 96])
class op111_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 12, p1: 8, p2: 8}
    index0 = 64*p0 + 8*p1 + p2
    index1 = p2
    index2 = 15
    index3 = 128*p0 + 15*p1 + p2 + 128*(((15*p1 + p2)//120)) + 8
    index4 = 8*p0 + 96*p1 + p2
    index5 = p1
    index6 = 8*p0 + p1
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf105', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        check_bounds = ops.check_bounds(get_index_1, get_index_2, False, False)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index3')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf107', get_index_3)
        add = ops.add(load, load_1)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index4')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf110', get_index_4)
        add_1 = ops.add(add, load_2)
        constant = ops.constant(0.125, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(add_1, constant)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_3 = ops.load('primals_3', get_index_5)
        to_dtype = ops.to_dtype(load_3, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        sub = ops.sub(constant_1, to_dtype)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(0.0, torch.float32)
        gt = ops.gt(sub, constant_2)
        to_dtype_1 = ops.to_dtype(gt, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.bool)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index5')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index_6, torch.uint8)
        get_index_7 = self.get_index('index1')
        index_expr_1 = ops.index_expr(get_index_7, torch.uint8)
        eq = ops.eq(index_expr, index_expr_1)
        to_dtype_2 = ops.to_dtype(eq, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.bool)
        neg = ops.neg(to_dtype_2)
        add_2 = ops.add(to_dtype_1, neg)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(0.0, torch.float32)
        gt_1 = ops.gt(add_2, constant_3)
        to_dtype_3 = ops.to_dtype(gt_1, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.bool)
        constant_4 = ops.constant(1e+30, torch.float32)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(to_dtype_3, constant_4)
        sub_1 = ops.sub(mul, mul_1)
        reduction = ops.reduction(torch.float32, torch.float32, 'online_softmax_reduce', sub_1)
        getitem = reduction[0]

    index6 = 8*p0 + p1
    def body(self, ops):

        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        check_bounds = ops.check_bounds(get_index_1, get_index_2, False, False)

primals_3_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.int64, size=[1, 8], stride=[8, 1])
buf235_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[1, 12, 8, 1], stride=[96, 8, 1, 96])
class op235_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 12, p1: 8, p2: 8}
    index0 = 64*p0 + 8*p1 + p2
    index1 = p2
    index2 = 15
    index3 = 128*p0 + 15*p1 + p2 + 128*(((15*p1 + p2)//120)) + 8
    index4 = 8*p0 + 96*p1 + p2
    index5 = p1
    index6 = 8*p0 + p1
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf229', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        check_bounds = ops.check_bounds(get_index_1, get_index_2, False, False)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index3')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf231', get_index_3)
        add = ops.add(load, load_1)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index4')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf234', get_index_4)
        add_1 = ops.add(add, load_2)
        constant = ops.constant(0.125, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(add_1, constant)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_3 = ops.load('primals_3', get_index_5)
        to_dtype = ops.to_dtype(load_3, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        sub = ops.sub(constant_1, to_dtype)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(0.0, torch.float32)
        gt = ops.gt(sub, constant_2)
        to_dtype_1 = ops.to_dtype(gt, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.bool)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index5')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index_6, torch.uint8)
        get_index_7 = self.get_index('index1')
        index_expr_1 = ops.index_expr(get_index_7, torch.uint8)
        eq = ops.eq(index_expr, index_expr_1)
        to_dtype_2 = ops.to_dtype(eq, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.bool)

    index5 = p1
    index6 = 8*p0 + p1

class op359_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 12, p1: 8, p2: 8}
    index0 = 64*p0 + 8*p1 + p2
    index1 = p2
    index2 = 15
    index3 = 128*p0 + 15*p1 + p2 + 128*(((15*p1 + p2)//120)) + 8
    index4 = 8*p0 + 96*p1 + p2
    index5 = p1
    index6 = 8*p0 + p1
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf353', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        check_bounds = ops.check_bounds(get_index_1, get_index_2, False, False)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index3')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf355', get_index_3)
        add = ops.add(load, load_1)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index4')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf358', get_index_4)
        add_1 = ops.add(add, load_2)
        constant = ops.constant(0.125, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(add_1, constant)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_3 = ops.load('primals_3', get_index_5)
        to_dtype = ops.to_dtype(load_3, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(1.0, torch.float32)
        sub = ops.sub(constant_1, to_dtype)
